-- FitMatch source product snapshot store + Uniqlo 880-row import
-- Generated: 2026-08-15
-- Safe to rerun: schema uses IF NOT EXISTS and snapshot rows are upserted.

begin;

create table if not exists fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs (
  id uuid primary key default gen_random_uuid(),
  source text not null check (source in ('uniqlo','musinsa')),
  run_kind text not null default 'scheduled' check (run_kind in ('scheduled','manual','document_import')),
  status text not null default 'queued' check (status in ('queued','running','succeeded','partial','failed')),
  snapshot_date date not null,
  mapping_release_id uuid references fitmatch_catalog.releases(id) on delete restrict,
  expected_count integer check (expected_count is null or expected_count >= 0),
  discovered_count integer not null default 0 check (discovered_count >= 0),
  stored_count integer not null default 0 check (stored_count >= 0),
  failed_count integer not null default 0 check (failed_count >= 0),
  cursor jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
  metadata jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
  error_summary text,
  started_at timestamptz,
  finished_at timestamptz,
  created_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  check (finished_at is null or started_at is null or finished_at >= started_at)
);

create table if not exists fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots (
  run_id uuid not null references fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs(id) on delete cascade,
  source text not null check (source in ('uniqlo','musinsa')),
  external_product_id text not null,
  product_name text not null,
  canonical_url text not null,
  audience text,
  observed_ids text[] not null default '{}'::text[],
  source_category_path text,
  source_category_codes text[] not null default '{}'::text[],
  fitmatch_category_label text,
  fitmatch_detail_label text,
  classification_status text not null check (classification_status in ('comparable','conditional','excluded_review','unclassified')),
  size_count integer check (size_count is null or size_count >= 0),
  image_url text,
  raw_summary jsonb not null default '{}'::jsonb,
  collected_at timestamptz not null default now(),
  primary key (run_id, external_product_id)
);

create index if not exists product_collection_runs_source_date_idx
  on fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs (source, snapshot_date desc, created_at desc);
create unique index if not exists product_collection_runs_document_import_uq
  on fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs (source, snapshot_date, run_kind)
  where run_kind = 'document_import';
create index if not exists source_product_snapshots_product_idx
  on fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots (source, external_product_id, collected_at desc);
create index if not exists source_product_snapshots_category_idx
  on fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots (run_id, fitmatch_category_label, fitmatch_detail_label);

alter table fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs enable row level security;
alter table fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots enable row level security;
revoke all on fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs from anon, authenticated;
revoke all on fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots from anon, authenticated;
grant select, insert, update, delete on fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs to service_role;
grant select, insert, update, delete on fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots to service_role;

create or replace view fitmatch_catalog.current_source_products
with (security_invoker = true)
as
select p.*
from fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots p
where p.run_id = (
  select r.id
  from fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs r
  where r.source = p.source and r.status in ('succeeded','partial')
  order by r.snapshot_date desc, r.created_at desc
  limit 1
);
revoke all on fitmatch_catalog.current_source_products from anon, authenticated;
grant select on fitmatch_catalog.current_source_products to service_role;

with imported_run as (
  insert into fitmatch_catalog.product_collection_runs (
    source, run_kind, status, snapshot_date, mapping_release_id,
    expected_count, discovered_count, stored_count, failed_count,
    started_at, finished_at, metadata
  ) values (
    'uniqlo', 'document_import', 'succeeded', date '2026-08-15',
    (select id from fitmatch_catalog.releases
     where release_key = 'observed-official-2026-08-03__taxonomy-corrected-2026-08-14'),
    880, 880, 880, 0, now(), now(),
    jsonb_build_object(
      'source_document','CurrentUniqloProductCategoryCatalog-20260815.md',
      'source_json','CurrentUniqloCatalogInputs.json',
      'scope','current_active_products'
    )
  )
  on conflict (source, snapshot_date, run_kind) where run_kind = 'document_import'
  do update set
    status = excluded.status,
    mapping_release_id = excluded.mapping_release_id,
    expected_count = excluded.expected_count,
    discovered_count = excluded.discovered_count,
    stored_count = excluded.stored_count,
    failed_count = excluded.failed_count,
    finished_at = excluded.finished_at,
    metadata = excluded.metadata
  returning id
), payload as (

as x(
    external_product_id text,
    product_name text,
    canonical_url text,
    audience text,
    observed_ids text[],
    source_category_path text,
    source_category_codes text[],
    fitmatch_category_label text,
    fitmatch_detail_label text,
    classification_status text,
    size_count integer,
    image_url text,
    raw_summary jsonb
  )
)
insert into fitmatch_catalog.source_product_snapshots (
  run_id, source, external_product_id, product_name, canonical_url, audience,
  observed_ids, source_category_path, source_category_codes,
  fitmatch_category_label, fitmatch_detail_label, classification_status,
  size_count, image_url, raw_summary
)
select r.id, 'uniqlo', p.external_product_id, p.product_name, p.canonical_url, p.audience,
       p.observed_ids, p.source_category_path, p.source_category_codes,
       p.fitmatch_category_label, p.fitmatch_detail_label, p.classification_status,
       p.size_count, p.image_url, p.raw_summary
from imported_run r cross join payload p
on conflict (run_id, external_product_id) do update set
  product_name = excluded.product_name,
  canonical_url = excluded.canonical_url,
  audience = excluded.audience,
  observed_ids = excluded.observed_ids,
  source_category_path = excluded.source_category_path,
  source_category_codes = excluded.source_category_codes,
  fitmatch_category_label = excluded.fitmatch_category_label,
  fitmatch_detail_label = excluded.fitmatch_detail_label,
  classification_status = excluded.classification_status,
  size_count = excluded.size_count,
  image_url = excluded.image_url,
  raw_summary = excluded.raw_summary,
  collected_at = now();

do $verify$
declare v_count integer;
begin
  select count(*) into v_count
  from fitmatch_catalog.current_source_products
  where source = 'uniqlo';
  if v_count <> 880 then
    raise exception 'Uniqlo import count mismatch: expected 880, got %', v_count;
  end if;
end
$verify$;

commit;

select source, count(*) as current_product_count,
       count(*) filter (where classification_status='comparable') as comparable,
       count(*) filter (where classification_status='conditional') as conditional,
       count(*) filter (where classification_status='excluded_review') as excluded_review,
       count(*) filter (where classification_status='unclassified') as unclassified
from fitmatch_catalog.current_source_products
group by source;
